\clearpage\section*{Complete intersection of multidegrees $(3,3)$ in $\mathbb P^{3}\times \mathbb P^{3}$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PROD5-B03}
A smooth complete intersection in $Y=\mathbb P^{3}\times\mathbb P^{3}$; the rows below are the defining multidegrees in the displayed factor order.
\begin{lstlisting}
multidegrees = [[3, 3]]
residual_product_degrees = [1, 1]
\end{lstlisting}
The residual product degrees give the anticanonical class.
\subsection*{Invariants and full Hodge diamond}
$\dim X=5$, $h^{1,1}=2$, index $1$, $(-K_X)^5=60$, $e(X)=-3492$.
\[\begin{array}{ccccccccccc}&&&&&1&&&&&\\&&&&0&&0&&&&\\&&&0&&2&&0&&&\\&&0&&0&&0&&0&&\\&0&&0&&3&&0&&0&\\0&&100&&1652&&1652&&100&&0\\&0&&0&&3&&0&&0&\\&&0&&0&&0&&0&&\\&&&0&&2&&0&&&\\&&&&0&&0&&&&\\&&&&&1&&&&&\end{array}\]
$h^{4,1}=100$, $h^{3,2}=1652$; even rank 12; odd rank 3504.
Independent K-theory check: $(\chi(\mathcal O_X),\chi(\Omega_X^1),\chi(\Omega_X^2))=(1, 98, -1649)$.

\end{lstlisting}

\clearpage\section*{Complete intersection of multidegrees $(3,3)$ in $\mathbb P^{3}\times \mathbb P^{3}$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{8}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 30.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{4} + 420 x^{3} - 528714 x^{2} + 106457220 x - 2729492559.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.
\subsection*{Numerical eigenvalues of $c_1\star$}
Values are rounded to eight significant digits; repeated eigenvalues are retained with multiplicity.
\begin{center}\small\begin{tabular}{rrr@{\qquad}rrr}\toprule $j$ & Real part & Imaginary part & $j$ & Real part & Imaginary part\\\midrule
1 & 1033.6277 & 0 & 7 & -30 & 0\\
2 & -291.81734 & -53.723755 & 8 & -30 & 0\\
3 & -291.81734 & 53.723755 & 9 & -30 & 0\\
4 & -30 & 0 & 10 & -30 & 0\\
5 & -30 & 0 & 11 & -30 & 0\\
6 & -30 & 0 & 12 & -29.993005 & 0\\
\bottomrule\end{tabular}\end{center}
\subsection*{Connection exponents}
\begin{center}\begin{tabular}{llll}\toprule Factor & Powers & Classes mod $\mathbb Z$ & Status / method\\\midrule
$p_{1}$ & unresolved & unresolved & not computed\\
$p_{2}$ & $0$ & $0$ & complete / residue\\
\bottomrule\end{tabular}\end{center}
Powers and residue classes are multisets for each eigenvalue in the indicated factor, not paired lists. A simple-factor power applies at every root of that factor.
\textbf{Unresolved factor $p_{1}$:} the eigenvalue 30 has a 3-dimensional kernel, so its generalized eigenspace is not a single Jordan block and the factor is not cyclic (check cyclic\_determinant first).

$\dim X=5$, $h^{1,1}=2$, index $1$, $(-K_X)^5=50$, $e(X)=-3678$.

\clearpage\section*{Complete intersection of multidegrees $(2,4)$ in $\mathbb P^{2}\times \mathbb P^{4}$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{7}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 54.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{5} + 1326 x^{4} - 19224 x^{3} + 192631280 x^{2} - 21539338416 x\\&+592705245024.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.
\subsection*{Numerical eigenvalues of $c_1\star$}
Values are rounded to eight significant digits; repeated eigenvalues are retained with multiplicity.
\begin{center}\small\begin{tabular}{rrr@{\qquad}rrr}\toprule $j$ & Real part & Imaginary part & $j$ & Real part & Imaginary part\\\midrule
1 & 1439.6519 & 0 & 7 & -54 & 0\\
2 & -2.7661522 & -375.32237 & 8 & -54 & 0\\
3 & -2.7661522 & 375.32237 & 9 & -54 & 0\\
4 & -54.059789 & -0.026120553 & 10 & -54 & 0\\
5 & -54.059789 & 0.026120553 & 11 & -54 & 0\\
6 & -54 & 0 & 12 & -54 & 0\\
\bottomrule\end{tabular}\end{center}
\subsection*{Connection exponents}
\begin{center}\begin{tabular}{llll}\toprule Factor & Powers & Classes mod $\mathbb Z$ & Status / method\\\midrule
$p_{1}$ & unresolved & unresolved & not computed\\
$p_{2}$ & $0$ & $0$ & complete / residue\\
\bottomrule\end{tabular}\end{center}
Powers and residue classes are multisets for each eigenvalue in the indicated factor, not paired lists. A simple-factor power applies at every root of that factor.
\textbf{Unresolved factor $p_{1}$:} the eigenvalue 54 has a 2-dimensional kernel, so its generalized eigenspace is not a single Jordan block and the factor is not cyclic (check cyclic\_determinant first).

\clearpage\section*{Quartic double cover of $\mathbb P^{5}$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:QD5-001}
Quartic double cover of $\mathbb P^5$ branched along a smooth quartic; equivalently $X_4\subset\mathbb P(1^6,2)$.
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^5}(\mathcal O\oplus\mathcal O(2))$, $E=\mathcal O_Y(2\xi)$ and $-K_X=4H$. The exceptional section misses $X$, and $\xi|_X=2H|_X$. The defining divisor and residual are nef on $Y$; ampleness on $X$ follows from the finite cover.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{3}}(\mathcal O^{3}\oplus \mathcal O(1))$ by $2\xi +2H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-036}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{3}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(1))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(2\xi+2H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=2\xi+1H$; the summands and residual are ample.
Workbook source ID: 36.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{3}}(\mathcal O^{4}\oplus \mathcal O(1))$ by $\xi +H$, $2\xi +H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-056}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{3}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(1))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+1H)\oplus\mathcal O_Y(2\xi+1H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=2\xi+1H$; the summands and residual are ample.
Workbook source ID: 56.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O^{2}\oplus \mathcal O(1))$ by $2\xi +2H$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{5}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 6.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{7} + 18 x^{6} + 100 x^{5} + 48072 x^{4} - 3600848 x^{3} + 64107360 x^{2}\\&+782745984 - 388798272 x.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O^{2}\oplus \mathcal O(2))$ by $2\xi +H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-079}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(2))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(2\xi+1H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=1\xi+2H$; the summands and residual are ample.
Workbook source ID: 79.
\subsection*{Invariants and full Hodge diamond}
$\dim X=5$, $h^{1,1}=2$, index $1$, $(-K_X)^5=1200$, $e(X)=-448$.

$h^{4,1}=5$, $h^{3,2}=225$; even rank 12; odd rank 460.
Independent K-theory check: $(\chi(\mathcal O_X),\chi(\Omega_X^1),\chi(\Omega_X^2))=(1, 3, -222)$.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O\oplus \mathcal O(1)^{2})$ by $2\xi +H$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{4}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 6.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{8} + 12 x^{7} - 80 x^{6} + 7308 x^{5} - 1392012 x^{4} + 37157184 x^{3}\\&- 355811292 x^{2} + 1405424520 x - 2062810476.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.

\begin{center}\begin{tabular}{llll}\toprule Factor & Powers & Classes mod $\mathbb Z$ & Status / method\\\midrule
$p_{1}$ & unresolved & unresolved & not computed\\

Powers and residue classes are multisets for each eigenvalue in the indicated factor, not paired lists. A simple-factor power applies at every root of that factor.
\textbf{Unresolved factor $p_{1}$:} the eigenvalue 6 has a 2-dimensional kernel, so its generalized eigenspace is not a single Jordan block and the factor is not cyclic (check cyclic\_determinant first).
\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O^{3}\oplus \mathcal O(1))$ by $\xi +H$, $2\xi +H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-101}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(1))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+1H)\oplus\mathcal O_Y(2\xi+1H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=1\xi+2H$; the summands and residual are ample.
Workbook source ID: 101.
\subsection*{Invariants and full Hodge diamond}
$\dim X=5$, $h^{1,1}=2$, index $1$, $(-K_X)^5=866$, $e(X)=-156$.

$h^{4,1}=1$, $h^{3,2}=83$; even rank 12; odd rank 168.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O^{2}\oplus \mathcal O(1))$ by $\xi +H$, $\xi +2H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-139}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(1))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+1H)\oplus\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+2H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=1\xi+2H$; the summands and residual are ample.
Workbook source ID: 139.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O^{2}\oplus \mathcal O(1))$ by $\xi +H$, $\xi +2H$ --- spectrum and connection}

\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{5}\,p_{2}(x).\]

\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{7} - 3 x^{6} + 64 x^{5} + 20376 x^{4} - 698240 x^{3} + 7277760 x^{2}\\&+22398336 - 17722368 x.\end{aligned}\]

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O\oplus \mathcal O(2))$ by $\xi +2H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-127}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(2))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+2H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=1\xi+2H$; the summands and residual are ample.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O\oplus \mathcal O(2))$ by $\xi +2H$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{4}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 2.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{6} - 192 x^{4} - 1536 x^{3} - 207360 x^{2} + 1492992 x\\&+35509248.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{3}}(\mathcal O^{3}\oplus \mathcal O(2))$ by $2\xi +H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-040}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{3}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(2))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(2\xi+1H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=2\xi+1H$; the summands and residual are ample.
Workbook source ID: 40.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O\oplus \mathcal O(4))$ by $\xi +H$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{4}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 2.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{6} + 768 x^{5} + 1920 x^{4} + 4920 x^{3} + 12960 x^{2} + 1866240 x\\&+1401771024.\end{aligned}\]

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O^{2}\oplus \mathcal O(2))$ by $2\xi +2H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-080}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(2))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(2\xi+2H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=1\xi+1H$; the summands and residual are ample.
Workbook source ID: 80.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O^{2}\oplus \mathcal O(3))$ by $\xi +H$, $\xi +H$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{6}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 2.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{6} - 162 x^{5} + 5841 x^{4} + 37584 x^{3} - 361800 x^{2} + 93848544 x\\&-3782279664.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.
\subsection*{Numerical eigenvalues of $c_1\star$}
Values are rounded to eight significant digits; repeated eigenvalues are retained with multiplicity.
\begin{center}\small\begin{tabular}{rrr@{\qquad}rrr}\toprule $j$ & Real part & Imaginary part & $j$ & Real part & Imaginary part\\\midrule
1 & -98.870569 & 0 & 7 & -2 & 0\\
2 & -69.359461 & 0 & 8 & -2 & 0\\
3 & 28.686151 & 0 & 9 & -2 & 0\\
4 & 1.5281999 & -27.409661 & 10 & -2 & 0\\
5 & 1.5281999 & 27.409661 & 11 & -2 & 0\\
6 & -25.51252 & 0 & 12 & -2 & 0\\
\bottomrule\end{tabular}\end{center}
\subsection*{Connection exponents}
\begin{center}\begin{tabular}{llll}\toprule Factor & Powers & Classes mod $\mathbb Z$ & Status / method\\\midrule
$p_{1}$ & unresolved & unresolved & not computed\\
$p_{2}$ & $0$ & $0$ & complete / residue\\
\bottomrule\end{tabular}\end{center}
Powers and residue classes are multisets for each eigenvalue in the indicated factor, not paired lists. A simple-factor power applies at every root of that factor.
\textbf{Unresolved factor $p_{1}$:} the eigenvalue 2 has a 3-dimensional kernel, so its generalized eigenspace is not a single Jordan block and the factor is not cyclic (check cyclic\_determinant first).
\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O\oplus \mathcal O(1)\oplus \mathcal O(2))$ by $\xi +H$, $\xi +H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-147}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(1)\oplus\mathcal O(2))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+1H)\oplus\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+1H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=1\xi+1H$; the summands and residual are ample.
Workbook source ID: 147.
\subsection*{Invariants and full Hodge diamond}
$\dim X=5$, $h^{1,1}=2$, index $1$, $(-K_X)^5=966$, $e(X)=-212$.
\[\begin{array}{ccccccccccc}&&&&&1&&&&&\\&&&&0&&0&&&&\\&&&0&&2&&0&&&\\&&0&&0&&0&&0&&\\&0&&0&&3&&0&&0&\\0&&2&&110&&110&&2&&0\\&0&&0&&3&&0&&0&\\&&0&&0&&0&&0&&\\&&&0&&2&&0&&&\\&&&&0&&0&&&&\\&&&&&1&&&&&\end{array}\]
$h^{4,1}=2$, $h^{3,2}=110$; even rank 12; odd rank 224.
Independent K-theory check: $(\chi(\mathcal O_X),\chi(\Omega_X^1),\chi(\Omega_X^2))=(1, 0, -107)$.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O^{3}\oplus \mathcal O(2))$ by $\xi +H$, $\xi +H$, $\xi +H$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{6}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 2.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{6} + 36 x^{5} + 1008 x^{4} + 113076 x^{3} + 2331936 x^{2} + 54849312 x\\&+35852004.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.

\begin{center}\begin{tabular}{llll}\toprule Factor & Powers & Classes mod $\mathbb Z$ & Status / method\\\midrule
$p_{1}$ & unresolved & unresolved & not computed\\

Powers and residue classes are multisets for each eigenvalue in the indicated factor, not paired lists. A simple-factor power applies at every root of that factor.
\textbf{Unresolved factor $p_{1}$:} the eigenvalue 2 has a 2-dimensional kernel, so its generalized eigenspace is not a single Jordan block and the factor is not cyclic (check cyclic\_determinant first).
\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O^{2}\oplus \mathcal O(2))$ by $\xi +H$, $\xi +2H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-143}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(2))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+1H)\oplus\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+2H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=1\xi+1H$; the summands and residual are ample.
Workbook source ID: 143.
\subsection*{Invariants and full Hodge diamond}
$\dim X=5$, $h^{1,1}=2$, index $1$, $(-K_X)^5=722$, $e(X)=-498$.

$h^{4,1}=7$, $h^{3,2}=248$; even rank 12; odd rank 510.
Independent K-theory check: $(\chi(\mathcal O_X),\chi(\Omega_X^1),\chi(\Omega_X^2))=(1, 5, -245)$.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O\oplus \mathcal O(3))$ by $\xi +2H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-130}

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O^{3}\oplus \mathcal O(2))$ by $\xi +H$, $2\xi +H$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{6}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 4.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{6} + 52 x^{5} + 1940 x^{4} + 482364 x^{3} + 4753444 x^{2}\\&+197331320 x - 121793164.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O^{2}\oplus \mathcal O(3))$ by $2\xi +H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-082}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(3))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(2\xi+1H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=1\xi+1H$; the summands and residual are ample.
Workbook source ID: 82.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O\oplus \mathcal O(1)\oplus \mathcal O(2))$ by $2\xi +H$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{6}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 6.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{6} + 16 x^{5} + 600 x^{4} + 430308 x^{3} - 25794612 x^{2}\\&+651619080 x - 5070641148.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.

Powers and residue classes are multisets for each eigenvalue in the indicated factor, not paired lists. A simple-factor power applies at every root of that factor.
\textbf{Unresolved factor $p_{1}$:} the eigenvalue 6 has a 2-dimensional kernel, so its generalized eigenspace is not a single Jordan block and the factor is not cyclic (check cyclic\_determinant first).
\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O\oplus \mathcal O(2))$ by $\xi +3H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-128}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(2))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+3H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=1\xi+1H$; the summands and residual are ample.
Workbook source ID: 128.

$h^{4,1}=21$, $h^{3,2}=566$; even rank 10; odd rank 1174.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{3}}(\mathcal O^{3}\oplus \mathcal O(2))$ by $3\xi +H$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{6}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 12.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{6} + 180 x^{5} + 13968 x^{4} + 4913460 x^{3} - 127115136 x^{2}\\&+2466868608 x - 13632905772.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O\oplus \mathcal O(1)^{2})$ by $2\xi +2H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-086}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(1)\oplus\mathcal O(1))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(2\xi+2H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=1\xi+1H$; the summands and residual are ample.
Workbook source ID: 86.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O^{3}\oplus \mathcal O(1))$ by $\xi +H$, $2\xi +2H$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{7}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 6.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{5} + 6 x^{4} - 29400 x^{3} + 3343792 x^{2} - 14593968 x\\&+18499296.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O^{3}\oplus \mathcal O(1))$ by $\xi +2H$, $2\xi +H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-103}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(1))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+2H)\oplus\mathcal O_Y(2\xi+1H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=1\xi+1H$; the summands and residual are ample.
Workbook source ID: 103.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O^{2}\oplus \mathcal O(1)^{2})$ by $\xi +H$, $\xi +H$, $\xi +H$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{6}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 3.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{6} + 4 x^{5} - 3116 x^{4} + 266052 x^{3} - 147072 x^{2} + 21578256 x\\&-63978300.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.

Values are rounded to eight significant digits; repeated eigenvalues are retained with multiplicity.

\subsection*{Connection exponents}
\begin{center}\begin{tabular}{llll}\toprule Factor & Powers & Classes mod $\mathbb Z$ & Status / method\\\midrule
$p_{1}$ & unresolved & unresolved & not computed\\
$p_{2}$ & $0$ & $0$ & complete / residue\\

Powers and residue classes are multisets for each eigenvalue in the indicated factor, not paired lists. A simple-factor power applies at every root of that factor.
\textbf{Unresolved factor $p_{1}$:} the eigenvalue 3 has a 2-dimensional kernel, so its generalized eigenspace is not a single Jordan block and the factor is not cyclic (check cyclic\_determinant first).
\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O^{4}\oplus \mathcal O(1))$ by $\xi +H$, $\xi +H$, $\xi +H$, $\xi +H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-158}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(1))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+1H)\oplus\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+1H)\oplus\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+1H)\oplus\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+1H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=1\xi+1H$; the summands and residual are ample.
Workbook source ID: 158.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O^{2}\oplus \mathcal O(1))$ by $2\xi +3H$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{7}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 6.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{5} - 78 x^{4} - 86304 x^{3} + 14792760 x^{2} - 275389308 x\\&+1138116744.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.
\subsection*{Numerical eigenvalues of $c_1\star$}
Values are rounded to eight significant digits; repeated eigenvalues are retained with multiplicity.

3 & -193.16412 & 46.790223 & 9 & -6 & 0\\

\subsection*{Connection exponents}
\begin{center}\begin{tabular}{llll}\toprule Factor & Powers & Classes mod $\mathbb Z$ & Status / method\\\midrule
$p_{1}$ & unresolved & unresolved & not computed\\
$p_{2}$ & $0$ & $0$ & complete / residue\\
\bottomrule\end{tabular}\end{center}
Powers and residue classes are multisets for each eigenvalue in the indicated factor, not paired lists. A simple-factor power applies at every root of that factor.
\textbf{Unresolved factor $p_{1}$:} the eigenvalue 6 has a 2-dimensional kernel, so its generalized eigenspace is not a single Jordan block and the factor is not cyclic (check cyclic\_determinant first).
\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O^{4}\oplus \mathcal O(1))$ by $\xi +H$, $\xi +H$, $2\xi +H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-114}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(1))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+1H)\oplus\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+1H)\oplus\mathcal O_Y(2\xi+1H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=1\xi+1H$; the summands and residual are ample.
Workbook source ID: 114.
\subsection*{Invariants and full Hodge diamond}
$\dim X=5$, $h^{1,1}=2$, index $1$, $(-K_X)^5=262$, $e(X)=-592$.
\[\begin{array}{ccccccccccc}&&&&&1&&&&&\\&&&&0&&0&&&&\\&&&0&&2&&0&&&\\&&0&&0&&0&&0&&\\&0&&0&&3&&0&&0&\\0&&11&&291&&291&&11&&0\\&0&&0&&3&&0&&0&\\&&0&&0&&0&&0&&\\&&&0&&2&&0&&&\\&&&&0&&0&&&&\\&&&&&1&&&&&\end{array}\]
$h^{4,1}=11$, $h^{3,2}=291$; even rank 12; odd rank 604.
Independent K-theory check: $(\chi(\mathcal O_X),\chi(\Omega_X^1),\chi(\Omega_X^2))=(1, 9, -288)$.

\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{6}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 6.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{6} + 16 x^{5} - 12540 x^{4} + 1451524 x^{3} - 20317208 x^{2}\\&+111914160 x - 237945132.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{3}}(\mathcal O^{3}\oplus \mathcal O(1))$ by $3\xi +2H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-038}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{3}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(1))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(3\xi+2H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=1\xi+1H$; the summands and residual are ample.
Workbook source ID: 38.

$h^{4,1}=74$, $h^{3,2}=1334$; even rank 12; odd rank 2816.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{3}}(\mathcal O^{3}\oplus \mathcal O(1))$ by $3\xi +2H$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{7}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 18.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{5} + 210 x^{4} - 73194 x^{3} + 28004920 x^{2} - 908942847 x\\&+7714310046.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{4}}(\mathcal O^{2}\oplus \mathcal O(1)^{2})$ by $\xi +H$, $2\xi +H$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{6}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 6.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{6} + 28 x^{5} - 4952 x^{4} + 1008804 x^{3} - 15881184 x^{2}\\&+159585120 x - 565387452.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{3}}(\mathcal O^{4}\oplus \mathcal O(1))$ by $\xi +H$, $3\xi +H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-057}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{3}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(1))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+1H)\oplus\mathcal O_Y(3\xi+1H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=1\xi+1H$; the summands and residual are ample.
Workbook source ID: 57.

$h^{4,1}=36$, $h^{3,2}=744$; even rank 12; odd rank 1560.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{3}}(\mathcal O^{4}\oplus \mathcal O(1))$ by $2\xi +H$, $2\xi +H$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{6}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 12.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{6} + 112 x^{5} - 17112 x^{4} + 4462936 x^{3} - 147214736 x^{2}\\&+1713207648 x - 6747718896.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.

\begin{center}\begin{tabular}{llll}\toprule Factor & Powers & Classes mod $\mathbb Z$ & Status / method\\\midrule
$p_{1}$ & unresolved & unresolved & not computed\\

Powers and residue classes are multisets for each eigenvalue in the indicated factor, not paired lists. A simple-factor power applies at every root of that factor.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{3}}(\mathcal O^{2}\oplus \mathcal O(1)^{2})$ by $3\xi +H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-044}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{3}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(1)\oplus\mathcal O(1))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(3\xi+1H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=1\xi+1H$; the summands and residual are ample.
Workbook source ID: 44.
\subsection*{Invariants and full Hodge diamond}
$\dim X=5$, $h^{1,1}=2$, index $1$, $(-K_X)^5=170$, $e(X)=-2052$.

$h^{4,1}=49$, $h^{3,2}=983$; even rank 12; odd rank 2064.
Independent K-theory check: $(\chi(\mathcal O_X),\chi(\Omega_X^1),\chi(\Omega_X^2))=(1, 47, -980)$.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{2}}(\mathcal O^{4}\oplus \mathcal O(1))$ by $4\xi +H$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{6}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 72.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{6} + 1864 x^{5} + 918552 x^{4} + 138707032 x^{3} - 65915386352 x^{2}\\&+5701207621728 x - 148984756257648.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.
\subsection*{Numerical eigenvalues of $c_1\star$}
Values are rounded to eight significant digits; repeated eigenvalues are retained with multiplicity.
\begin{center}\small\begin{tabular}{rrr@{\qquad}rrr}\toprule $j$ & Real part & Imaginary part & $j$ & Real part & Imaginary part\\\midrule
1 & 1256.3119 & 0 & 7 & -72 & 0\\
2 & 411.86739 & -384.54883 & 8 & -72 & 0\\
3 & 411.86739 & 384.54883 & 9 & -72 & 0\\
4 & -72.248153 & 0 & 10 & -72 & 0\\
5 & -72 & 0 & 11 & -71.899246 & -0.30962824\\
6 & -72 & 0 & 12 & -71.899246 & 0.30962824\\
\bottomrule\end{tabular}\end{center}
\subsection*{Connection exponents}
\begin{center}\begin{tabular}{llll}\toprule Factor & Powers & Classes mod $\mathbb Z$ & Status / method\\\midrule
$p_{1}$ & unresolved & unresolved & not computed\\
$p_{2}$ & $0$ & $0$ & complete / residue\\
\bottomrule\end{tabular}\end{center}
Powers and residue classes are multisets for each eigenvalue in the indicated factor, not paired lists. A simple-factor power applies at every root of that factor.
\textbf{Unresolved factor $p_{1}$:} the eigenvalue 72 has a 2-dimensional kernel, so its generalized eigenspace is not a single Jordan block and the factor is not cyclic (check cyclic\_determinant first).

$h^{4,1}=25$, $h^{3,2}=521$; even rank 12; odd rank 1092.

\clearpage\section*{Complete intersection of multidegrees $(2,2)$, $(1,1)$, $(1,1)$ in $\mathbb P^{4}\times \mathbb P^{4}$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{7}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 10.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{5} + 50 x^{4} - 59000 x^{3} + 5410000 x^{2} - 85150000 x\\&+372100000.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.

\clearpage\section*{Complete intersection of multidegrees $(2,1)$, $(1,2)$, $(1,1)$ in $\mathbb P^{4}\times \mathbb P^{4}$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{6}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 10.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{6} + 40 x^{5} - 40456 x^{4} + 3436380 x^{3} - 79886516 x^{2}\\&+726083000 x - 2308999372.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.
\subsection*{Numerical eigenvalues of $c_1\star$}
Values are rounded to eight significant digits; repeated eigenvalues are retained with multiplicity.
\begin{center}\small\begin{tabular}{rrr@{\qquad}rrr}\toprule $j$ & Real part & Imaginary part & $j$ & Real part & Imaginary part\\\midrule
1 & 255.71203 & 0 & 7 & -10 & 0\\
2 & -92.717389 & -13.697531 & 8 & -10 & 0\\
3 & -92.717389 & 13.697531 & 9 & -10 & 0\\
4 & -10.14293 & 0 & 10 & -10 & 0\\
5 & -10.096649 & 0 & 11 & -10 & 0\\
6 & -10.03767 & 0 & 12 & -10 & 0\\
\bottomrule\end{tabular}\end{center}
\subsection*{Connection exponents}
\begin{center}\begin{tabular}{llll}\toprule Factor & Powers & Classes mod $\mathbb Z$ & Status / method\\\midrule
$p_{1}$ & unresolved & unresolved & not computed\\
$p_{2}$ & $0$ & $0$ & complete / residue\\
\bottomrule\end{tabular}\end{center}
Powers and residue classes are multisets for each eigenvalue in the indicated factor, not paired lists. A simple-factor power applies at every root of that factor.
\textbf{Unresolved factor $p_{1}$:} the eigenvalue 10 has a 2-dimensional kernel, so its generalized eigenspace is not a single Jordan block and the factor is not cyclic (check cyclic\_determinant first).

\clearpage\section*{Complete intersection of multidegrees $(2,3)$, $(1,1)$ in $\mathbb P^{3}\times \mathbb P^{4}$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{7}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 18.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{5} + 186 x^{4} - 157212 x^{3} + 23873544 x^{2} - 710255196 x\\&+5945038200.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.
\subsection*{Numerical eigenvalues of $c_1\star$}
Values are rounded to eight significant digits; repeated eigenvalues are retained with multiplicity.
\begin{center}\small\begin{tabular}{rrr@{\qquad}rrr}\toprule $j$ & Real part & Imaginary part & $j$ & Real part & Imaginary part\\\midrule
1 & 552.79102 & 0 & 7 & -18 & 0\\
2 & -165.43492 & -77.267395 & 8 & -18 & 0\\
3 & -165.43492 & 77.267395 & 9 & -18 & 0\\
4 & -18 & 0 & 10 & -18 & 0\\
5 & -18 & 0 & 11 & -17.960593 & -0.013974018\\
6 & -18 & 0 & 12 & -17.960593 & 0.013974018\\
\bottomrule\end{tabular}\end{center}
\subsection*{Connection exponents}
\begin{center}\begin{tabular}{llll}\toprule Factor & Powers & Classes mod $\mathbb Z$ & Status / method\\\midrule
$p_{1}$ & unresolved & unresolved & not computed\\
$p_{2}$ & $0$ & $0$ & complete / residue\\
\bottomrule\end{tabular}\end{center}
Powers and residue classes are multisets for each eigenvalue in the indicated factor, not paired lists. A simple-factor power applies at every root of that factor.
\textbf{Unresolved factor $p_{1}$:} the eigenvalue 18 has a 2-dimensional kernel, so its generalized eigenspace is not a single Jordan block and the factor is not cyclic (check cyclic\_determinant first).

\clearpage\section*{Complete intersection of multidegrees $(2,2)$, $(1,2)$ in $\mathbb P^{3}\times \mathbb P^{4}$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{7}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 14.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{5} + 118 x^{4} - 87864 x^{3} + 9147888 x^{2} - 206522928 x\\&+1334372064.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.
\subsection*{Numerical eigenvalues of $c_1\star$}
Values are rounded to eight significant digits; repeated eigenvalues are retained with multiplicity.
\begin{center}\small\begin{tabular}{rrr@{\qquad}rrr}\toprule $j$ & Real part & Imaginary part & $j$ & Real part & Imaginary part\\\midrule
1 & 398.98695 & 0 & 7 & -14 & 0\\
2 & -126.44373 & -30.898339 & 8 & -14 & 0\\
3 & -126.44373 & 30.898339 & 9 & -14 & 0\\
4 & -14.078613 & 0 & 10 & -14 & 0\\
5 & -14.020875 & 0 & 11 & -14 & 0\\
6 & -14 & 0 & 12 & -14 & 0\\
\bottomrule\end{tabular}\end{center}
\subsection*{Connection exponents}
\begin{center}\begin{tabular}{llll}\toprule Factor & Powers & Classes mod $\mathbb Z$ & Status / method\\\midrule
$p_{1}$ & unresolved & unresolved & not computed\\
$p_{2}$ & $0$ & $0$ & complete / residue\\
\bottomrule\end{tabular}\end{center}
Powers and residue classes are multisets for each eigenvalue in the indicated factor, not paired lists. A simple-factor power applies at every root of that factor.
\textbf{Unresolved factor $p_{1}$:} the eigenvalue 14 has a 3-dimensional kernel, so its generalized eigenspace is not a single Jordan block and the factor is not cyclic (check cyclic\_determinant first).

\clearpage\section*{Complete intersection of multidegrees $(2,2,2)$ in $\mathbb P^{2}\times \mathbb P^{2}\times \mathbb P^{2}$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3,\ y_3=5$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{11}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 20.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{9} + 180 x^{8} - 25776 x^{7} + 4896960 x^{6} - 444907008 x^{5}\\&+36841328640 x^{4} - 1967691890688 x^{3} + 85755764736000 x^{2}\\&+16119716249600000 - 1971756564480000 x.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.
\subsection*{Numerical eigenvalues of $c_1\star$}
Values are rounded to eight significant digits; repeated eigenvalues are retained with multiplicity.

\subsection*{Connection exponents}
\begin{center}\begin{tabular}{llll}\toprule Factor & Powers & Classes mod $\mathbb Z$ & Status / method\\\midrule
$p_{1}$ & unresolved & unresolved & not computed\\

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O^{3}\oplus \mathcal O(1))$ by $\xi +H$, $\xi +H$, $\xi +2H$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-153}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(1))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+1H)\oplus\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+1H)\oplus\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+2H)$ defines $X$.

\clearpage\section*{Section of $\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{5}}(\mathcal O^{3}\oplus \mathcal O(1))$ by $\xi +H$, $\xi +H$, $\xi +2H$ --- spectrum and connection}

\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{6}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 3.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{6} - 24 x^{5} - 12300 x^{4} + 792604 x^{3} - 813636 x^{2} - 1086264 x\\&-9781452.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.

\clearpage\section*{Complete intersection of multidegrees $(2,2)$ in $\mathbb P^{3}\times \mathbb P^{3}$ --- spectrum and connection}

\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{4}\,p_{2}(x).\]

\clearpage\section*{Complete intersection of multidegrees $(2,1)$, $(1,2)$ in $\mathbb P^{3}\times \mathbb P^{4}$ --- construction and Hodge data}\label{entry:PB5-051}
In the quotient convention, $Y=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^{3}}(\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0)\oplus\mathcal O(0))$.
A general regular section of $E=\mathcal O_Y(1\xi+2H)\oplus\mathcal O_Y(2\xi+1H)$ defines $X$.
Here $H$ is the pullback of the hyperplane class and $\xi$ the tautological quotient class. The residual is $A=2\xi+1H$; the summands and residual are ample.

\clearpage\section*{Complete intersection of multidegrees $(1,1)$, $(1,1)$, $(1,2)$ in $\mathbb P^{3}\times \mathbb P^{5}$ --- spectrum and connection}
\textbf{Specialization:} $y_1=2,\ y_2=3$. The connection operator is $K^F=-M$. The exact factors below refer to $K^F$; the numerical eigenvalues refer to $M=c_1\star$.
\subsection*{Exact characteristic polynomial of $K^F$}
\[\det(xI-K^F)=p_{1}(x)^{4}\,p_{2}(x).\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{1}(x)&=x - 3.\end{aligned}\]
\[\begin{aligned}p_{2}(x)&=x^{8} - 6 x^{7} - 119 x^{6} + 41472 x^{5} - 1069284 x^{4} + 11655300 x^{3}\\&- 54907592 x^{2} + 117054912 x - 94049852.\end{aligned}\]
Cyclic determinant: $\Delta=0$. The specialized operator is not cyclic.
\subsection*{Numerical eigenvalues of $c_1\star$}
Values are rounded to eight significant digits; repeated eigenvalues are retained with multiplicity.
\begin{center}\small\begin{tabular}{rrr@{\qquad}rrr}\toprule $j$ & Real part & Imaginary part & $j$ & Real part & Imaginary part\\\midrule
1 & 41.184913 & 0 & 7 & -3 & 0\\
2 & -10.242699 & -29.705651 & 8 & -3 & 0\\
3 & -10.242699 & 29.705651 & 9 & -3 & 0\\
4 & -9.4776575 & -5.7242748 & 10 & -2.5710095 & -0.80006499\\
5 & -9.4776575 & 5.7242748 & 11 & -2.5710095 & 0.80006499\\
6 & -3 & 0 & 12 & -2.6021806 & 0\\
\bottomrule\end{tabular}\end{center}
\subsection*{Connection exponents}
\begin{center}\begin{tabular}{llll}\toprule Factor & Powers & Classes mod $\mathbb Z$ & Status / method\\\midrule
$p_{1}$ & unresolved & unresolved & not computed\\
$p_{2}$ & $0$ & $0$ & complete / residue\\
\bottomrule\end{tabular}\end{center}
Powers and residue classes are multisets for each eigenvalue in the indicated factor, not paired lists. A simple-factor power applies at every root of that factor.
\textbf{Unresolved factor $p_{1}$:} the eigenvalue 3 has a 2-dimensional kernel, so its generalized eigenspace is not a single Jordan block and the factor is not cyclic (check cyclic\_determinant first).
